# Scenario Inventory

Every acceptance scenario the product promises, one row each, with the test that proves it. This
table answers "how far have we tested?". How to add a row and how to name a test with its ID:
`testing.md` § Scenario Inventory. Check it with `node scripts/check-scenarios.mjs`.

Drafted from `prd.md` (user journeys, roles, cross-cutting rules, notifications),
`fe-client-features.md`, `admin-restructure.md`, `class-booking-lifecycle.md` (with
`spec-pre-launch-batch.md` §1–§3), `pt-session-lifecycle.md`, `spec-finance.md`,
`spec-credit-ledger-payroll.md`, `spec-instructor-leave.md`, `spec-instructor-leave-pools.md` and
`spec-instructor-leave-remediation.md`. Each scenario ends with its sources in italics. Where a doc
and today's code disagree, the row states what the code does. A row starting **Not built.** is a
promise whose route still answers `501`.

**IDs are stable.** Never renumber or reuse one. A new row takes the next number in its area; a
dropped scenario stays, marked `wont-test`, with the reason in its cell.

## Columns

- **ID** — area prefix and number, e.g. `CHK-03`.
- **area** — the prefix, one of:
  `AUTH` sign-up, login, verification, password reset ·
  `WVR` waivers ·
  `CAT` public catalogue & marketing pages ·
  `BKG` class booking ·
  `PKR` package rules: which packages a class or series accepts ·
  `WTL` waitlist ·
  `SEAT` class seats: online, buffer, overbook, attendance capacity ·
  `CXL` class cancellation & cancellation policy ·
  `CHK` check-in & per-booking QR ·
  `ROS` roster & session detail ·
  `SCH` schedule, class instances, series, capacity, rooms, class types ·
  `WSP` workshops ·
  `PT` private sessions & PT requests ·
  `PKG` packages, validity, Families, promotions ·
  `CRD` credit ledger ·
  `PAY` checkout & payments ·
  `RFD` refunds ·
  `PRM` promo codes ·
  `MRC` merch ·
  `CORP` corporate ·
  `REF` referral ·
  `INV` member invoices & purchases ·
  `ACC` member account ·
  `CUS` admin customers ·
  `STF` staff, roles & invitations ·
  `LOC` locations & workspaces ·
  `NTF` notifications & email templates ·
  `INB` inbox ·
  `FIN` finance ·
  `PAYR` payroll ·
  `LEV` instructor leave ·
  `RPT` reports ·
  `SUP` super portal ·
  `TEN` Tenant isolation ·
  `AUD` audit trail
  An area with no rows yet (`REF`, `INV`, `RPT`: their routes are unbuilt or the specs defer them)
  has no section.
- **role** — whose action it is: `member` (including an anonymous visitor), `instructor`, `admin`,
  `super`, or `system` (a scheduled job, a webhook, or a rule with no human actor).
- **scenario** — **Given** / **When** / **Then**, then _(sources)_.
- **risk** — the worst outcome if it breaks: `money`, `tenancy`, `data-loss` or `UX`.
- **level** — the lowest level that can prove it: `unit`, `integration` (HTTP or service against
  real Postgres) or `e2e` (a real browser).
- **covered by** — the test file(s) with a test named with this ID, comma-separated.
- **status** — `uncovered`, `covered`, `failing` (the test exists and is red) or `wont-test`.

## SEAT — Class seats

A class has online seats members book and buffer seats only staff fill; attendance capacity is their
sum, and the waitlist is not a seat (`spec-waitlist.md` §1–§2).

| ID | area | role | scenario | risk | level | covered by | status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SEAT-01 | SEAT | admin | **Given** a class with a free buffer seat **When** an admin or the class's instructor books a member onto it **Then** the booking takes a buffer seat, is paid by the member's package like any booking, and no online seat is taken: the member catalogue still offers one _(spec-waitlist §2, §7)_ | money | integration | `be/src/test/class-seats.test.ts`, `be/src/services/bookings/seats.test.ts` | covered |
| SEAT-02 | SEAT | admin | **Given** a class whose buffer seats are all taken **When** staff book another member **Then** it is refused `class_full` with the waitlist fields; an admin who asks to overbook gets an overbook seat, and an instructor asking the same is still refused _(spec-waitlist §2, §7)_ | money | integration | `be/src/test/class-seats.test.ts`, `be/src/services/bookings/seats.test.ts` | covered |
| SEAT-03 | SEAT | system | **Given** a class with a waiting member **When** a buffer or overbook booking on it is cancelled **Then** nobody is promoted from the waitlist _(spec-waitlist §2)_ | UX | integration | `be/src/test/waitlist.test.ts` | covered |
| SEAT-04 | SEAT | admin | **Given** a class with online, buffer and overbook bookings **When** its session detail, the portal timetable and the member catalogue are read **Then** they agree: booked reads attending over attendance capacity, and the member catalogue offers a seat exactly while online seats taken are fewer than online capacity, whatever the buffer holds _(spec-waitlist §2, §10)_ | UX | integration | `be/src/test/class-seats.test.ts` | covered |
| SEAT-05 | SEAT | admin | **Given** two studios with bookings on their own classes **When** one studio reads a class's seats, or its staff book a member **Then** the other studio's bookings never count toward its seats, its waiting entries never count toward or get promoted into this studio's line, and neither the other studio's class nor its member can be booked _(spec-waitlist §2, §5)_ | tenancy | integration | `be/src/test/class-seats.test.ts`, `be/src/test/waitlist.test.ts` | covered |

| ID | area | role | scenario | risk | level | covered by | status |

| RFD-16 | RFD | member | **Given** a member's Purchase with upcoming bookings on its package **When** it is refunded, from the portal or the provider's dashboard **Then** the member gets one `purchase_refunded` email saying the package is refunded in full for the amount and no longer covers bookings, and naming each upcoming booking it cancelled (or saying none were) _(fe-client-features §7; #128 client-scenarios draft REF-01, REF-03)_ | UX | integration | `be/src/test/refunds.test.ts` | covered |
| RFD-17 | RFD | system | **Given** a refunded Purchase whose `charge.refunded` has been handled **When** the payment provider delivers that event again **Then** it is acknowledged and nothing changes: the Purchase, its payments, the package and its ledger stay as the first delivery left them _(fe-client-features §7; #128 client-scenarios draft REF-05)_ | money | integration | `be/src/test/refunds.test.ts` | covered |

| PRM-10 | PRM | admin | **Given** an Admin creating a Promo Code **When** they leave the code blank, type one in lower case with stray spaces, or type one already used at this studio **Then** a code of 8 characters from the unambiguous alphabet is generated, the typed code is stored trimmed and upper-cased, and the duplicate is refused _(spec-pre-launch-batch §10)_ | money | integration | `be/src/test/admin-catalogue.test.ts` | covered |

| PRM-15 | PRM | member | **Given** a Promo Code that exists only at another studio **When** a member types it at checkout **Then** it is refused with "We don't recognise that code" and the other studio's code is untouched _(spec-pre-launch-batch §9, §11)_ | tenancy | integration | `be/src/test/promo-codes-and-merch.test.ts` | covered |

| SUP-23 | SUP | super | **Given** a studio whose member already has a password **When** it is replaced from an export with passwords that holds that member with another password and a newcomer **Then** the member still signs in with their own password and not the archive's, and the newcomer signs in with the archive's _(ADR 0006 § Export with passwords; #339)_ | data-loss | integration | `be/src/test/tenant-export-passwords.test.ts` | covered |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| AUD-01 | AUD | admin | **Given** a signed-in Admin **When** a state-changing request (credit adjustment, class cancel, package grant) succeeds **Then** an audit row is written under the studio naming the actor, action, target and time _(prd §3.9)_ | data-loss | integration |  | uncovered |
| AUD-02 | AUD | admin | **Given** an Admin impersonating a member **When** a state-changing action is taken in the impersonated session **Then** the audit row names the Admin as the actor and records the impersonated member _(prd §2.4)_ | data-loss | integration | `be/src/test/impersonation.test.ts` | covered |

| AUD-04 | AUD | admin | **Given** an approved leave request **When** an admin revokes it **Then** the audit log records the revoking admin as the actor of the revoke transition _(spec-instructor-leave-remediation §Revocation preserves the approval)_ | data-loss | integration |  | uncovered |
| AUD-05 | AUD | instructor | **Given** a class cancelled by its main instructor **When** the cancellation is recorded **Then** its cancellation source is instructor, not admin, and an audit log entry is written _(spec-instructor-leave §Instructor-initiated class cancellation)_ | UX | integration |  | uncovered |
